- [[governance-and-reporting]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron emphasized that implementation without governance is not enough."
- [[governance-and-reporting]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "When advisors present a tax-aware transition analysis to a client, the numbers can hit hard."
- [[grandmother-effect]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "“I always call this the grandmother effect. Would I put my grandmother in that fund?”"
- [[groundwork-principle]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "Influential policy change doesn't happen during the frenzied legislative process. It is the result of years of prior education, modeling, and relationship-building with policymakers and their staff."
- [[high-turnover-fund]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Asset location strategy: Ideal for private credit, high-turnover funds (short-term gains), and even public strategies to avoid taxable distributions during turbulence."
- [[household-balance-sheet]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "How do we look across the full family balance sheet?"
- [[household-balance-sheet]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The household balance sheet is the complete view of the family’s assets across accounts, tax statuses, entities, and goals."
- [[household-level-diversification]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Qualification is measured at the household level. The rules are: largest position ≤ 25% of contributed assets, top five positions ≤ 50%."
- [[idiosyncratic-risk]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Concentrated wealth brings all the same tax issues as other transitions, but adds a major layer: **idiosyncratic risk**."
- [[implementation-layer]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The real power, according to Aaron, lies in the implementation layer."
- [[in-kind-creationredemption]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Core idea: Approval of Bitcoin ETFs creates a bridge from crypto natives to traditional finance via in-kind creations."
- [[information-ratio]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "He framed this in information-ratio terms, noting that if the after-tax benefit is reliable, the trade can be very attractive."
- [[information-ratio]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Information ratio (IR) varied from -1 to +1 to reflect manager skill."
- [[integrated-portfolio-management]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The central message is that true tax optimization is not about isolated tactics but about a holistic, systematic process that considers every component—equities, bonds, and cash flows—working in concert."
- [[is-the-client-comfortable-restructuring-the-balance-sheet]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax-aware long-short can require a major restructuring of the household’s equity exposure."
- [[is-the-client-comfortable-with-complexity]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "### 3. Is the Client Comfortable With Complexity?"
- [[is-the-client-comfortable-with-complexity]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Some clients will never be comfortable with gross exposure above 100%, shorting, leverage, or the complexity of the structure."
- [[is-there-a-use-for-the-losses]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Generating losses is only valuable if the client has a plan for them."
- [[is-there-a-use-for-the-losses]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "He offered four questions advisors should ask before using such a strategy:"
- [[laddered-diversification]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Core idea: Combine immediate sales, layered VPFs (often January 1 settlements), and tax-aware long-short to hedge now and shield future taxes."
- [[look-through-basis]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "ETFs contributed are assessed by their underlying holdings. This enables swapping one ETF for another via a 351."
- [[look-through-process]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The **ossified portfolio** is one that is not broken enough to sell, but too tax-burdened to easily fix."
- [[losses-are-inventory]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "As Aaron stated, **“Losses are inventory.”**"
- [[losses-are-inventory]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The second principle was one of the talk’s most memorable ideas: **losses are inventory**."
- [[lot-level-implementation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "implement client-specific portfolios at the tax-lot level."
- [[lot-level-implementation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Lot-level implementation means making decisions at the individual tax-lot level rather than at the fund or model level."
- [[market-beta]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Core idea: Market beta alone doesn’t change percentage leverage; alpha does."
- [[mean-reversion]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "Markowitz quote evolution: first “diversification is the only free lunch,” later “diversification and mean reversion.”"
- [[monetizing-volatility]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "As Aaron explained, **“Inventorying losses is monetizing volatility.”**"
- [[monetizing-volatility]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Inventorying losses is a way of **monetizing volatility**."
- [[muni-optimization-challenge]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "Scale: Over a million CUSIPs exist."
- [[muni-optimization-challenge]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The speaker detailed why muni optimization is so hard:"
- [[nav-accrual]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Method: In bonds (e.g., AGG, high yield), NAV accrues daily income, but tax impact only occurs on receipt of dividends."
- [[nav]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "mutual funds and ETFs can share a single portfolio, same NAV, same rights."
- [[negative-correlation-of-shorts]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Shorts are negatively correlated to the market, creating loss-harvesting opportunities in both up and down markets."
- [[no-cash-flow-assumption]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "This was especially important because Aaron noted that many academic studies on tax-loss harvesting assume no cash flows. In real life, that assumption is false."
- [[not-my-company]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "But Aaron warned that the client’s eyes may glaze over because they believe: “Yeah, but not my company. I helped build this.”"
- [[octane]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Match the long-short “octane” (leverage/active risk) to free cash while generating losses aligned to forward maturities."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The antidote is intelligent wrappers and innovation that make the investor always win."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The firm pioneered dual share class structures that deliver ETF trading efficiency to mutual fund buyers and is pushing forward with tokenization to collapse operational costs, tighten spreads, and unlock 24/7 liquidity—all while preserving or improving tax outcomes."
- [[ossified-portfolio]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The **ossified portfolio** is one that is not broken enough to sell, but too tax-burdened to easily fix."
- [[otc-collar]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "As Speaker 2 said: “The more tax-efficient way is a single OTC collar that fuses the put and the call into one instrument that can’t be separated.”"
- [[own-securities-where-control-matters]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "As Aaron framed the design principle, **“Own securities where the control matters.”**"
- [[own-securities-where-control-matters]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The first principle is to **own securities where control matters**."
- [[pending-liquidity-event]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron described this as “unbelievably powerful.”"
- [[percentage-leverage]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Core idea: Market beta alone doesn’t change percentage leverage; alpha does."
- [[permanent-tax-avoidance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The speaker reveals that unlike equities, where tax loss harvesting is primarily a *deferral* strategy, bond tax loss harvesting can achieve permanent *tax avoidance*."
- [[persistence-of-tax-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "“The persistence of tax alpha in fixed income tax managed strategies is high. It does not ossify over time because you get... new cost basis.”"
- [[policy-formation-map]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Core idea: Policy formation is complicated, slow, and often temporary; significant reform hasn’t happened since the mid-1980s."
- [[pooled-vehicle]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Mutual funds and other commingled products can generate unpredictable taxable distributions."
- [[pooled-vehicle]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The investor may owe taxes even if they did not sell anything personally."
- [[portfolio-lock-up]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "In contrast, higher alpha in long-only direct indexing tends to lock portfolios up—raising built-in gains and reducing tax-loss harvesting opportunities."
- [[portfolio-risk]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax decisions are never purely tax decisions. Selling, holding, transitioning, or hedging all change the risk profile."
- [[position-creation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Definition: Alpha-driven under-leverage in long-short that necessitates adding fresh long and short positions to restore target leverage."
- [[pre-tax-product-selection]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The keynote’s central theme was the evolution of wealth management from **pre-tax product selection** to **after-tax portfolio design**."
- [[private-placement-life-insurance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Core idea: PPLI solves the “back half” of the tax problem—deferring taxes during accumulation, enabling tax-free distribution at death."
- [[property-tax-repeal]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "On the right, some states are exploring the radical idea of repealing property taxes."
- [[proxy-fund]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Strategy avoids dividend capture, rotates into proxy funds around ex-dates, then reverses—collecting price appreciation while minimizing distributions."
- [[qualified-business-income-deduction]] → [[wiki/sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "**QBI Deduction:** Investors can deduct 20% of dividends earned via the Qualified Business Income (QBI) deduction, which is now a permanent part of the tax code."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[wiki/sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "A skilled manager can construct a portfolio that delivers a high percentage of its distribution as QDI, making its taxable equivalent yield extremely attractive."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[wiki/sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "She explained that about half of the preferreds universe throws off Qualified Dividend Income (QDI), which is taxed at a much lower rate (0-20%)."
- [[r&d-expensing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "This chapter details the more recent, multi-year campaign to make key pro-growth policies permanent, specifically the full deductibility of R&D and capital investment costs, which were initially left with sunset clauses in the TCJA."
- [[realized-gains-and-losses]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron made an important distinction: **tax drag is not a single problem**. It is a collection of problems that compound into complexity."
- [[rebalancing-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "When the market is wrong, this systematic contrarian rebalancing earns **rebalancing alpha.**"
- [[rebalancing-destination]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "Once the trigger is hit (e.g., at 66.5% equity), you don't rebalance all the way back to 60%."
- [[rebalancing-tax-drag]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The friction of rebalancing creates a significant, unmeasured tax drag that can be drastically reduced by managing the portfolio holistically, not in sleeves."
- [[research-gap]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Despite a robust literature on taxes and portfolio mechanics, no prior work had directly measured how pretax alpha affects tax outcomes in long-short contexts."
- [[rolling-15-year-simulation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "This keynote fills that gap with 21 rolling 15-year simulations and a framework that redefines how investors should evaluate manager skill: alpha is not just return—it’s a tax engine."
- [[sawtooth-pattern]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Analogy: From “sawtooth” dividend pattern to smoothed total return."
- [[section-1031-exchange]] → [[wiki/sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "Casey highlighted that this can be used for various properties (except a primary home) and even for situations like natural disaster losses (1033 exchange)."
- [[section-351-exchange]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "A Section 351 exchange offers a clean, compliant path to swap these appreciated holdings into a diversified ETF without triggering taxes, deferring gains while achieving broad market exposure."
- [[sector-and-country-distortion-under-cap-weights]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "Dot-com bubble and AI surge show tech ballooning in cap-weighted portfolios."
- [[short-term-mean-reversion]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "“There is such a thing as too much of a good thing. Harvesting every day, for example, exposes your portfolio to short-term mean reversion.”"
- [[shorting-against-the-box]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax-aware long-short can require a major restructuring of the household’s equity exposure."
- [[siloed-sleeve-management]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "“Advisors would call me and say, 'Why is there cash in the portfolio? This is a muni bond portfolio. You are supposed to own bonds, not cash.'” (Illustrating the old, siloed mindset)."
- [[simple-etf-portfolios]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The key argument was that clients can already build simple ETF portfolios themselves."
- [[smart-beta]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "They all fail after tax. Welcome to the real world."
- [[soft-rebalancing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "This is the practice of rebalancing using the portfolio's natural cash flows (dividends, bond coupons, maturities) instead of triggering taxable sales."
- [[stage-sales]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron was clear that modern transition management is not about “ripping the Band-Aid off.” It is not merely about staging sales over time."
- [[state-level-tax-experiments]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "Monitor State-Level Tax Experiments Closely. The speaker warned that radical partisan ideas are being tested in the states (e.g., repealing property taxes in Indiana/Florida, wealth taxes in CA/WA)."
- [[step-up-basis]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Key terminology: wrappers, tax alpha, dual share class, 351/1031, AMT, tokenized T-bills, in-kind creation/redemption, step-up basis, “CPMG/CPHY”, “proxy fund”, “sawtooth pattern”"
- [[straddle-rules]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Straddle rules (post‑1984 stock): Call premium taxable short-term currently; put losses non-deductible, added to basis until shares are sold."
- [[structural-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "As Speaker 4 said: “At the end of the day, the benefit is what we call structural alpha.”"
- [[sunset-clause]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "This chapter details the more recent, multi-year campaign to make key pro-growth policies permanent, specifically the full deductibility of R&D and capital investment costs, which were initially left with sunset clauses in the TCJA."
- [[synthetic-rebalancing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "A strategy for holders of large stock positions (e.g., pre-IPO shares) to use options to collar their position, borrow against it at low synthetic rates, and immediately diversify into other assets, all while deferring the initial tax bill."
- [[tax-advantaged-private-real-estate]] → [[wiki/sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "The principles of tax-aware investing extend deeply into private markets, particularly real estate, which offers unique and timely opportunities for tax deferral and income generation."
- [[tax-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "The takeaway is very simple: tax alpha matters. It matters hugely."
- [[tax-aware-deposits-and-withdrawals]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "This was especially important because Aaron noted that many academic studies on tax-loss harvesting assume no cash flows. In real life, that assumption is false."
- [[tax-aware-long-short-implementation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Select managers with demonstrable pretax alpha because alpha powers tax outcomes."
- [[tax-aware-public-market-solutions]] → [[wiki/sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "Before diving into complex alternatives, it's crucial to master the foundational elements of after-tax investing."
- [[tax-breakeven-equation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "As an over-the-counter market, round-trip costs must be factored into the tax-breakeven equation."
- [[tax-design]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "**Tax design** is the deliberate, proactive, engineered approach to minimizing tax drag and improving family-level outcomes."
- [[tax-drag]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "**Tax drag** is the difference between the pre-tax return of an asset or portfolio and the after-tax return the client actually experiences."
- [[tax-engine]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "This keynote fills that gap with 21 rolling 15-year simulations and a framework that redefines how investors should evaluate manager skill: alpha is not just return—it’s a tax engine."
- [[tax-free-policy-loan]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Practicalities: Institutionally priced, skinny policies; no surrender charges; liquidity mirrors underlying assets; tax-free policy loans during life."
- [[tax-loss-harvesting]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax-loss harvesting is often discussed mechanically, but Aaron reframed it more powerfully."
- [[tax-neutral-transitions]] → [[wiki/sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "By using tools like those developed by Brevan Howard and now part of Nuveen, we can create customized, tax-neutral transitions that de-risk a portfolio, defer or offset massive tax bills, and methodically move a client toward their ideal asset allocation."
- [[tax-overlay]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Active models with overlays: meaningful tax alpha when tax overlay is added"
- [[tax-overlay]] → [[_Inbox/at this tax conference Keynote_ After-Tax Wealth Management and Tax Stack Design-Summary (1)]]: "Build a solution inside the after-tax implementation ecosystem. Automate as much as possible. Allow the advisor to use judgment on which levers to pull."
- [[tax-overlay]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "ETF and fund models: approximately 50 to 100 basis points in tax alpha"
- [[tax-policy-sunsets]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Constraints: Sunsets via draconian parliamentary procedures; typically one year’s notice—not enough for full re-architecture."
- [[tax-stack]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "It is about building an **after-tax implementation platform** — what he called a **“tax stack”** — that allows advisors to diagnose tax drag, design around the family balance sheet, and implement client-specific portfolios at the tax-lot level."
- [[tax-trap]] → [[_Inbox/at this tax conference Keynote The Tax Trap in Bond Investing]]: "The overarching theme: The traditional, static 'set it and forget it' approach to fixed income investing is deeply flawed and leaves significant after-tax returns on the table."
- [[taxable-equivalent-yield]] → [[wiki/sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "A skilled manager can construct a portfolio that delivers a high percentage of its distribution as QDI, making its taxable equivalent yield extremely attractive."

- [[disallowed-losses]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron noted that shorting against the box can trigger onerous consequences if not handled correctly, including disallowed losses."

- [[fama–french-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "Framing: this tax alpha is **on top of** ~**2% Fama–French alpha** (as described)."

- [[no-cash-flow-assumption]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "This was especially important because Aaron noted that many academic studies on tax-loss harvesting assume no cash flows. - [[not-my-company]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "But Aaron warned that the client’s eyes may glaze over because they believe: “Yeah, but not my company. I helped build this.”"
- [[octane]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Match the long-short “octane” (leverage/active risk) to free cash while generating losses aligned to forward maturities."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The antidote is intelligent wrappers and innovation that make the investor always win."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The firm pioneered dual share class structures that deliver ETF trading efficiency to mutual fund buyers and is pushing forward with tokenization to collapse operational costs, tighten spreads, and unlock 24/7 liquidity—all while preserving or improving tax outcomes."
- [[ossified-portfolio]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The **ossified portfolio** is one that is not broken enough to sell, but too tax-burdened to easily fix."
- [[otc-collar]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "As Speaker 2 said: “The more tax-efficient way is a single OTC collar that fuses the put and the call into one instrument that can’t be separated.”"
- [[own-securities-where-control-matters]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "As Aaron framed the design principle, **“Own securities where the control matters.”**"
- [[own-securities-where-control-matters]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The first principle is to **own securities where control matters**."
- [[pending-liquidity-event]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron described this as “unbelievably powerful.”"
- [[percentage-leverage]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Core idea: Market beta alone doesn’t change percentage leverage; alpha does."
- [[permanent-tax-avoidance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The speaker reveals that unlike equities, where tax loss harvesting is primarily a *deferral* strategy, bond tax loss harvesting can achieve permanent *tax avoidance*."
- [[persistence-of-tax-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "“The persistence of tax alpha in fixed income tax managed strategies is high. It does not ossify over time because you get... new cost basis.”"
- [[policy-formation-map]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Core idea: Policy formation is complicated, slow, and often temporary; significant reform hasn’t happened since the mid-1980s."
- [[pooled-vehicle]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Mutual funds and other commingled products can generate unpredictable taxable distributions."
- [[pooled-vehicle]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The investor may owe taxes even if they did not sell anything personally."
- [[portfolio-lock-up]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "In contrast, higher alpha in long-only direct indexing tends to lock portfolios up—raising built-in gains and reducing tax-loss harvesting opportunities."
- [[portfolio-risk]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax decisions are never purely tax decisions. Selling, holding, transitioning, or hedging all change the risk profile."
- [[position-creation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Definition: Alpha-driven under-leverage in long-short that necessitates adding fresh long and short positions to restore target leverage."
- [[pre-tax-product-selection]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The keynote’s central theme was the evolution of wealth management from **pre-tax product selection** to **after-tax portfolio design**."
- [[private-placement-life-insurance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Core idea: PPLI solves the “back half” of the tax problem—deferring taxes during accumulation, enabling tax-free distribution at death."
- [[property-tax-repeal]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "On the right, some states are exploring the radical idea of repealing property taxes."
- [[proxy-fund]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Strategy avoids dividend capture, rotates into proxy funds around ex-dates, then reverses—collecting price appreciation while minimizing distributions."
- [[qualified-business-income-deduction]] → [[wiki/sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "**QBI Deduction:** Investors can deduct 20% of dividends earned via the Qualified Business Income (QBI) deduction, which is now a permanent part of the tax code."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "A skilled manager can construct a portfolio that delivers a high percentage of its distribution as QDI, making its taxable equivalent yield extremely attractive."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "She explained that about half of the preferreds universe throws off Qualified Dividend Income (QDI), which is taxed at a much lower rate (0-20%)."
- [[r&d-expensing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "This chapter details the more recent, multi-year campaign to make key pro-growth policies permanent, specifically the full deductibility of R&D and capital investment costs, which were initially left with sunset clauses in the TCJA."
- [[realized-gains-and-losses]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron made an important distinction: **tax drag is not a single problem**. It is a collection of problems that compound into complexity."
- [[rebalancing-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "When the market is wrong, this systematic contrarian rebalancing earns **rebalancing alpha.**"
- [[rebalancing-destination]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "Once the trigger is hit (e.g., at 66.5% equity), you don't rebalance all the way back to 60%."
- [[rebalancing-tax-drag]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The friction of rebalancing creates a significant, unmeasured tax drag that can be drastically reduced by managing the portfolio holistically, not in sleeves."
- [[research-gap]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Despite a robust literature on taxes and portfolio mechanics, no prior work had directly measured how pretax alpha affects tax outcomes in long-short contexts."

- [[not-my-company]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "But Aaron warned that the client’s eyes may glaze over because they believe: “Yeah, but not my company. I helped build this.”"
- [[octane]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Match the long-short “octane” (leverage/active risk) to free cash while generating losses aligned to forward maturities."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The antidote is intelligent wrappers and innovation that make the investor always win."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The firm pioneered dual share class structures that deliver ETF trading efficiency to mutual fund buyers and is pushing forward with tokenization to collapse operational costs, tighten spreads, and unlock 24/7 liquidity—all while preserving or improving tax outcomes."
- [[ossified-portfolio]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The **ossified portfolio** is one that is not broken enough to sell, but too tax-burdened to easily fix."
- [[otc-collar]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "As Speaker 2 said: “The more tax-efficient way is a single OTC collar that fuses the put and the call into one instrument that can’t be separated.”"
- [[own-securities-where-control-matters]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "As Aaron framed the design principle, **“Own securities where the control matters.”**"
- [[own-securities-where-control-matters]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The first principle is to **own securities where control matters**."
- [[pending-liquidity-event]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron described this as “unbelievably powerful.”"
- [[percentage-leverage]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Core idea: Market beta alone doesn’t change percentage leverage; alpha does."
- [[permanent-tax-avoidance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The speaker reveals that unlike equities, where tax loss harvesting is primarily a *deferral* strategy, bond tax loss harvesting can achieve permanent *tax avoidance*."
- [[persistence-of-tax-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "“The persistence of tax alpha in fixed income tax managed strategies is high. It does not ossify over time because you get... new cost basis.”"

- [[portfolio-risk]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax decisions are never purely tax decisions. Selling, holding, transitioning, or hedging all change the risk profile."
- [[position-creation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Definition: Alpha-driven under-leverage in long-short that necessitates adding fresh long and short positions to restore target leverage."
- [[pre-tax-product-selection]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The keynote’s central theme was the evolution of wealth management from **pre-tax product selection** to **after-tax portfolio design**."
- [[private-placement-life-insurance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Core idea: PPLI solves the “back half” of the tax problem—deferring taxes during accumulation, enabling tax-free distribution at death."
- [[property-tax-repeal]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "On the right, some states are exploring the radical idea of repealing property taxes."

- [[qualified-business-income-deduction]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-real-estate-strategies-summary]]: "**QBI Deduction:** Investors can deduct 20% of dividends earned via the Qualified Business Income (QBI) deduction, which is now a permanent part of the tax code."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "A skilled manager can construct a portfolio that delivers a high percentage of its distribution as QDI, making its taxable equivalent yield extremely attractive."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "She explained that about half of the preferreds universe throws off Qualified Dividend Income (QDI), which is taxed at a much lower rate (0-20%)."
- [[r&d-expensing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "This chapter details the more recent, multi-year campaign to make key pro-growth policies permanent, specifically the full deductibility of R&D and capital investment costs, which were initially left with sunset clauses in the TCJA."

- [[rebalancing-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "When the market is wrong, this systematic contrarian rebalancing earns **rebalancing alpha.**"
- [[rebalancing-destination]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "Once the trigger is hit (e.g., at 66.5% equity), you don't rebalance all the way back to 60%."

- [[research-gap]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Despite a robust literature on taxes and portfolio mechanics, no prior work had directly measured how pretax alpha affects tax outcomes in long-short contexts."

- [[governance-and-reporting]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "When advisors present a tax-aware transition analysis to a client, the numbers can hit hard."
- [[grandmother-effect]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "“I always call this the grandmother effect. Would I put my grandmother in that fund?”"

- [[household-balance-sheet]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "How do we look across the full family balance sheet?"
- [[household-balance-sheet]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The household balance sheet is the complete view of the family’s assets across accounts, tax statuses, entities, and goals."
- [[household-level-diversification]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Qualification is measured at the household level. The rules are: largest position ≤ 25% of contributed assets, top five positions ≤ 50%."
- [[idiosyncratic-risk]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Concentrated wealth brings all the same tax issues as other transitions, but adds a major layer: **idiosyncratic risk**."

- [[information-ratio]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "He framed this in information-ratio terms, noting that if the after-tax benefit is reliable, the trade can be very attractive."
- [[information-ratio]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Information ratio (IR) varied from -1 to +1 to reflect manager skill."
- [[integrated-portfolio-management]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The central message is that true tax optimization is not about isolated tactics but about a holistic, systematic process that considers every component—equities, bonds, and cash flows—working in concert."

- [[is-the-client-comfortable-with-complexity]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "### 3. Is the Client Comfortable With Complexity?"
- [[is-the-client-comfortable-with-complexity]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Some clients will never be comfortable with gross exposure above 100%, shorting, leverage, or the complexity of the structure."

- [[not-my-company]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "But Aaron warned that the client’s eyes may glaze over because they believe: “Yeah, but not my company. I helped build this.”"
- [[octane]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Match the long-short “octane” (leverage/active risk) to free cash while generating losses aligned to forward maturities."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The antidote is intelligent wrappers and innovation that make the investor always win."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The firm pioneered dual share class structures that deliver ETF trading efficiency to mutual fund buyers and is pushing forward with tokenization to collapse operational costs, tighten spreads, and unlock 24/7 liquidity—all while preserving or improving tax outcomes."
- [[ossified-portfolio]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The **ossified portfolio** is one that is not broken enough to sell, but too tax-burdened to easily fix."
- [[otc-collar]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "As Speaker 2 said: “The more tax-efficient way is a single OTC collar that fuses the put and the call into one instrument that can’t be separated.”"

- [[own-securities-where-control-matters]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The first principle is to **own securities where control matters**."

- [[permanent-tax-avoidance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The speaker reveals that unlike equities, where tax loss harvesting is primarily a *deferral* strategy, bond tax loss harvesting can achieve permanent *tax avoidance*."
- [[persistence-of-tax-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "“The persistence of tax alpha in fixed income tax managed strategies is high. It does not ossify over time because you get... new cost basis.”"
- [[policy-formation-map]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Core idea: Policy formation is complicated, slow, and often temporary; significant reform hasn’t happened since the mid-1980s."

- [[portfolio-lock-up]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "In contrast, higher alpha in long-only direct indexing tends to lock portfolios up—raising built-in gains and reducing tax-loss harvesting opportunities."
- [[portfolio-risk]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax decisions are never purely tax decisions. Selling, holding, transitioning, or hedging all change the risk profile."
- [[position-creation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Definition: Alpha-driven under-leverage in long-short that necessitates adding fresh long and short positions to restore target leverage."
- [[pre-tax-product-selection]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The keynote’s central theme was the evolution of wealth management from **pre-tax product selection** to **after-tax portfolio design**."
- [[private-placement-life-insurance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Core idea: PPLI solves the “back half” of the tax problem—deferring taxes during accumulation, enabling tax-free distribution at death."
- [[property-tax-repeal]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "On the right, some states are exploring the radical idea of repealing property taxes."
- [[proxy-fund]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Strategy avoids dividend capture, rotates into proxy funds around ex-dates, then reverses—collecting price appreciation while minimizing distributions."
- [[qualified-business-income-deduction]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "**QBI Deduction:** Investors can deduct 20% of dividends earned via the Qualified Business Income (QBI) deduction, which is now a permanent part of the tax code."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "A skilled manager can construct a portfolio that delivers a high percentage of its distribution as QDI, making its taxable equivalent yield extremely attractive."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "She explained that about half of the preferreds universe throws off Qualified Dividend Income (QDI), which is taxed at a much lower rate (0-20%)."
- [[r&d-expensing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "This chapter details the more recent, multi-year campaign to make key pro-growth policies permanent, specifically the full deductibility of R&D and capital investment costs, which were initially left with sunset clauses in the TCJA."

- [[rebalancing-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "When the market is wrong, this systematic contrarian rebalancing earns **rebalancing alpha.**"
- [[rebalancing-destination]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "Once the trigger is hit (e.g., at 66.5% equity), you don't rebalance all the way back to 60%."
- [[rebalancing-tax-drag]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The friction of rebalancing creates a significant, unmeasured tax drag that can be drastically reduced by managing the portfolio holistically, not in sleeves."
- [[research-gap]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Despite a robust literature on taxes and portfolio mechanics, no prior work had directly measured how pretax alpha affects tax outcomes in long-short contexts."
- [[rolling-15-year-simulation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "This keynote fills that gap with 21 rolling 15-year simulations and a framework that redefines how investors should evaluate manager skill: alpha is not just return—it’s a tax engine."
- [[sawtooth-pattern]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Analogy: From “sawtooth” dividend pattern to smoothed total return."
- [[section-1031-exchange]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "Casey highlighted that this can be used for various properties (except a primary home) and even for situations like natural disaster losses (1033 exchange)."
- [[section-351-exchange]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "A Section 351 exchange offers a clean, compliant path to swap these appreciated holdings into a diversified ETF without triggering taxes, deferring gains while achieving broad market exposure."

- [[siloed-sleeve-management]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "“Advisors would call me and say, 'Why is there cash in the portfolio? - [[straddle-rules]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Straddle rules (post‑1984 stock): Call premium taxable short-term currently; put losses non-deductible, added to basis until shares are sold."

- [[sunset-clause]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "This chapter details the more recent, multi-year campaign to make key pro-growth policies permanent, specifically the full deductibility of R&D and capital investment costs, which were initially left with sunset clauses in the TCJA."
- [[synthetic-rebalancing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "A strategy for holders of large stock positions (e.g., pre-IPO shares) to use options to collar their position, borrow against it at low synthetic rates, and immediately diversify into other assets, all while deferring the initial tax bill."

- [[tax-aware-long-short-implementation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Select managers with demonstrable pretax alpha because alpha powers tax outcomes."
- [[tax-aware-public-market-solutions]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "Before diving into complex alternatives, it's crucial to master the foundational elements of after-tax investing."
- [[tax-breakeven-equation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "As an over-the-counter market, round-trip costs must be factored into the tax-breakeven equation."
- [[tax-design]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "**Tax design** is the deliberate, proactive, engineered approach to minimizing tax drag and improving family-level outcomes."
- [[tax-drag]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "**Tax drag** is the difference between the pre-tax return of an asset or portfolio and the after-tax return the client actually experiences."
- [[tax-engine]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "This keynote fills that gap with 21 rolling 15-year simulations and a framework that redefines how investors should evaluate manager skill: alpha is not just return—it’s a tax engine."
- [[tax-free-policy-loan]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Practicalities: Institutionally priced, skinny policies; no surrender charges; liquidity mirrors underlying assets; tax-free policy loans during life."
- [[tax-loss-harvesting]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax-loss harvesting is often discussed mechanically, but Aaron reframed it more powerfully."

- [[portfolio-risk]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax decisions are never purely tax decisions. Selling, holding, transitioning, or hedging all change the risk profile."

- [[pre-tax-product-selection]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The keynote’s central theme was the evolution of wealth management from **pre-tax product selection** to **after-tax portfolio design**."
- [[private-placement-life-insurance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Core idea: PPLI solves the “back half” of the tax problem—deferring taxes during accumulation, enabling tax-free distribution at death."
- [[property-tax-repeal]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "On the right, some states are exploring the radical idea of repealing property taxes."
- [[proxy-fund]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Strategy avoids dividend capture, rotates into proxy funds around ex-dates, then reverses—collecting price appreciation while minimizing distributions."
- [[qualified-business-income-deduction]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "**QBI Deduction:** Investors can deduct 20% of dividends earned via the Qualified Business Income (QBI) deduction, which is now a permanent part of the tax code."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "A skilled manager can construct a portfolio that delivers a high percentage of its distribution as QDI, making its taxable equivalent yield extremely attractive."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "She explained that about half of the preferreds universe throws off Qualified Dividend Income (QDI), which is taxed at a much lower rate (0-20%)."
- [[r&d-expensing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "This chapter details the more recent, multi-year campaign to make key pro-growth policies permanent, specifically the full deductibility of R&D and capital investment costs, which were initially left with sunset clauses in the TCJA."
- [[realized-gains-and-losses]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron made an important distinction: **tax drag is not a single problem**. It is a collection of problems that compound into complexity."
- [[rebalancing-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "When the market is wrong, this systematic contrarian rebalancing earns **rebalancing alpha.**"
- [[rebalancing-destination]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "Once the trigger is hit (e.g., at 66.5% equity), you don't rebalance all the way back to 60%."
- [[rebalancing-tax-drag]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The friction of rebalancing creates a significant, unmeasured tax drag that can be drastically reduced by managing the portfolio holistically, not in sleeves."

- [[octane]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Match the long-short “octane” (leverage/active risk) to free cash while generating losses aligned to forward maturities."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The antidote is intelligent wrappers and innovation that make the investor always win."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The firm pioneered dual share class structures that deliver ETF trading efficiency to mutual fund buyers and is pushing forward with tokenization to collapse operational costs, tighten spreads, and unlock 24/7 liquidity—all while preserving or improving tax outcomes."

- [[otc-collar]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "As Speaker 2 said: “The more tax-efficient way is a single OTC collar that fuses the put and the call into one instrument that can’t be separated.”"
- [[own-securities-where-control-matters]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "As Aaron framed the design principle, **“Own securities where the control matters.”**"
- [[own-securities-where-control-matters]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The first principle is to **own securities where control matters**."

- [[permanent-tax-avoidance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The speaker reveals that unlike equities, where tax loss harvesting is primarily a *deferral* strategy, bond tax loss harvesting can achieve permanent *tax avoidance*."

- [[portfolio-risk]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax decisions are never purely tax decisions. Selling, holding, transitioning, or hedging all change the risk profile."
- [[position-creation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Definition: Alpha-driven under-leverage in long-short that necessitates adding fresh long and short positions to restore target leverage."
- [[pre-tax-product-selection]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The keynote’s central theme was the evolution of wealth management from **pre-tax product selection** to **after-tax portfolio design**."
- [[private-placement-life-insurance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Core idea: PPLI solves the “back half” of the tax problem—deferring taxes during accumulation, enabling tax-free distribution at death."
- [[property-tax-repeal]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "On the right, some states are exploring the radical idea of repealing property taxes."

- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "A skilled manager can construct a portfolio that delivers a high percentage of its distribution as QDI, making its taxable equivalent yield extremely attractive."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "She explained that about half of the preferreds universe throws off Qualified Dividend Income (QDI), which is taxed at a much lower rate (0-20%)."
- [[r&d-expensing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "This chapter details the more recent, multi-year campaign to make key pro-growth policies permanent, specifically the full deductibility of R&D and capital investment costs, which were initially left with sunset clauses in the TCJA."

- [[rebalancing-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "When the market is wrong, this systematic contrarian rebalancing earns **rebalancing alpha.**"
- [[rebalancing-destination]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "Once the trigger is hit (e.g., at 66.5% equity), you don't rebalance all the way back to 60%."
- [[rebalancing-tax-drag]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The friction of rebalancing creates a significant, unmeasured tax drag that can be drastically reduced by managing the portfolio holistically, not in sleeves."
- [[research-gap]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Despite a robust literature on taxes and portfolio mechanics, no prior work had directly measured how pretax alpha affects tax outcomes in long-short contexts."

- [[five-principles-for-passive-tax-loss-strategies]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The fourth principle was that transitions are roadmaps."
- [[full-cost-basis]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Why it matters: New positions come with full cost basis, increasing future loss harvesting."
- [[fundamental-growth]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "**Fundamental Growth (from “Trifecta”):** Instead of defining growth as “expensive,” select companies by **actual growth rates**, then weight by **dollar magnitude of growth**—framed as a superior way to build growth exposure."
- [[fundamental-indexing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "Fundamental index rule: wait until the company’s **business is big enough to matter**, then **weight by fundamental size** (economic footprint), not market cap."
- [[gain-deferral]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Long-only: defer gains, realize losses within tracking error."
- [[governance-and-reporting]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron emphasized that implementation without governance is not enough."
- [[governance-and-reporting]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "When advisors present a tax-aware transition analysis to a client, the numbers can hit hard."
- [[grandmother-effect]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "“I always call this the grandmother effect. Would I put my grandmother in that fund?”"
- [[groundwork-principle]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "Influential policy change doesn't happen during the frenzied legislative process. - [[high-turnover-fund]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Asset location strategy: Ideal for private credit, high-turnover funds (short-term gains), and even public strategies to avoid taxable distributions during turbulence."
- [[household-balance-sheet]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "How do we look across the full family balance sheet?"
- [[household-balance-sheet]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The household balance sheet is the complete view of the family’s assets across accounts, tax statuses, entities, and goals."
- [[household-level-diversification]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Qualification is measured at the household level. The rules are: largest position ≤ 25% of contributed assets, top five positions ≤ 50%."
- [[idiosyncratic-risk]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Concentrated wealth brings all the same tax issues as other transitions, but adds a major layer: **idiosyncratic risk**."
- [[implementation-layer]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The real power, according to Aaron, lies in the implementation layer."
- [[in-kind-creationredemption]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Core idea: Approval of Bitcoin ETFs creates a bridge from crypto natives to traditional finance via in-kind creations."
- [[information-ratio]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "He framed this in information-ratio terms, noting that if the after-tax benefit is reliable, the trade can be very attractive."
- [[information-ratio]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Information ratio (IR) varied from -1 to +1 to reflect manager skill."
- [[integrated-portfolio-management]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The central message is that true tax optimization is not about isolated tactics but about a holistic, systematic process that considers every component—equities, bonds, and cash flows—working in concert."
- [[is-the-client-comfortable-restructuring-the-balance-sheet]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax-aware long-short can require a major restructuring of the household’s equity exposure."
- [[is-the-client-comfortable-with-complexity]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "### 3. Is the Client Comfortable With Complexity?"
- [[is-the-client-comfortable-with-complexity]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Some clients will never be comfortable with gross exposure above 100%, shorting, leverage, or the complexity of the structure."
- [[is-there-a-use-for-the-losses]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Generating losses is only valuable if the client has a plan for them."
- [[is-there-a-use-for-the-losses]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "He offered four questions advisors should ask before using such a strategy:"

- [[look-through-basis]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "ETFs contributed are assessed by their underlying holdings. This enables swapping one ETF for another via a 351."
- [[look-through-process]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The **ossified portfolio** is one that is not broken enough to sell, but too tax-burdened to easily fix."
- [[losses-are-inventory]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "As Aaron stated, **“Losses are inventory.”**"
- [[losses-are-inventory]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The second principle was one of the talk’s most memorable ideas: **losses are inventory**."
- [[lot-level-implementation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "implement client-specific portfolios at the tax-lot level."
- [[lot-level-implementation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Lot-level implementation means making decisions at the individual tax-lot level rather than at the fund or model level."
- [[market-beta]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Core idea: Market beta alone doesn’t change percentage leverage; alpha does."

- [[monetizing-volatility]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "As Aaron explained, **“Inventorying losses is monetizing volatility.”**"

- [[nav-accrual]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Method: In bonds (e.g., AGG, high yield), NAV accrues daily income, but tax impact only occurs on receipt of dividends."
- [[nav]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "mutual funds and ETFs can share a single portfolio, same NAV, same rights."
- [[negative-correlation-of-shorts]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Shorts are negatively correlated to the market, creating loss-harvesting opportunities in both up and down markets."
- [[no-cash-flow-assumption]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "This was especially important because Aaron noted that many academic studies on tax-loss harvesting assume no cash flows.
