- [[not-my-company]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "But Aaron warned that the client’s eyes may glaze over because they believe: “Yeah, but not my company. I helped build this.”"
- [[octane]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Match the long-short “octane” (leverage/active risk) to free cash while generating losses aligned to forward maturities."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The antidote is intelligent wrappers and innovation that make the investor always win."
- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The firm pioneered dual share class structures that deliver ETF trading efficiency to mutual fund buyers and is pushing forward with tokenization to collapse operational costs, tighten spreads, and unlock 24/7 liquidity—all while preserving or improving tax outcomes."
- [[ossified-portfolio]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The **ossified portfolio** is one that is not broken enough to sell, but too tax-burdened to easily fix."
- [[otc-collar]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "As Speaker 2 said: “The more tax-efficient way is a single OTC collar that fuses the put and the call into one instrument that can’t be separated.”"
- [[own-securities-where-control-matters]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "As Aaron framed the design principle, **“Own securities where the control matters.”**"
- [[own-securities-where-control-matters]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The first principle is to **own securities where control matters**."
- [[pending-liquidity-event]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron described this as “unbelievably powerful.”"
- [[percentage-leverage]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Core idea: Market beta alone doesn’t change percentage leverage; alpha does."
- [[permanent-tax-avoidance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The speaker reveals that unlike equities, where tax loss harvesting is primarily a *deferral* strategy, bond tax loss harvesting can achieve permanent *tax avoidance*."
- [[persistence-of-tax-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "“The persistence of tax alpha in fixed income tax managed strategies is high. It does not ossify over time because you get... new cost basis.”"
- [[policy-formation-map]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Core idea: Policy formation is complicated, slow, and often temporary; significant reform hasn’t happened since the mid-1980s."
- [[pooled-vehicle]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Mutual funds and other commingled products can generate unpredictable taxable distributions."
- [[pooled-vehicle]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The investor may owe taxes even if they did not sell anything personally."
- [[portfolio-lock-up]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "In contrast, higher alpha in long-only direct indexing tends to lock portfolios up—raising built-in gains and reducing tax-loss harvesting opportunities."
- [[portfolio-risk]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax decisions are never purely tax decisions. Selling, holding, transitioning, or hedging all change the risk profile."
- [[position-creation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Definition: Alpha-driven under-leverage in long-short that necessitates adding fresh long and short positions to restore target leverage."
- [[pre-tax-product-selection]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The keynote’s central theme was the evolution of wealth management from **pre-tax product selection** to **after-tax portfolio design**."
- [[private-placement-life-insurance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Core idea: PPLI solves the “back half” of the tax problem—deferring taxes during accumulation, enabling tax-free distribution at death."
- [[property-tax-repeal]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "On the right, some states are exploring the radical idea of repealing property taxes."
- [[proxy-fund]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Strategy avoids dividend capture, rotates into proxy funds around ex-dates, then reverses—collecting price appreciation while minimizing distributions."
- [[qualified-business-income-deduction]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "**QBI Deduction:** Investors can deduct 20% of dividends earned via the Qualified Business Income (QBI) deduction, which is now a permanent part of the tax code."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "A skilled manager can construct a portfolio that delivers a high percentage of its distribution as QDI, making its taxable equivalent yield extremely attractive."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "She explained that about half of the preferreds universe throws off Qualified Dividend Income (QDI), which is taxed at a much lower rate (0-20%)."
- [[r&d-expensing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "This chapter details the more recent, multi-year campaign to make key pro-growth policies permanent, specifically the full deductibility of R&D and capital investment costs, which were initially left with sunset clauses in the TCJA."
- [[realized-gains-and-losses]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron made an important distinction: **tax drag is not a single problem**. It is a collection of problems that compound into complexity."
- [[rebalancing-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "When the market is wrong, this systematic contrarian rebalancing earns **rebalancing alpha.**"
- [[rebalancing-destination]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "Once the trigger is hit (e.g., at 66.5% equity), you don't rebalance all the way back to 60%."
- [[rebalancing-tax-drag]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The friction of rebalancing creates a significant, unmeasured tax drag that can be drastically reduced by managing the portfolio holistically, not in sleeves."
- [[research-gap]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Despite a robust literature on taxes and portfolio mechanics, no prior work had directly measured how pretax alpha affects tax outcomes in long-short contexts."
- [[rolling-15-year-simulation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "This keynote fills that gap with 21 rolling 15-year simulations and a framework that redefines how investors should evaluate manager skill: alpha is not just return—it’s a tax engine."
- [[sawtooth-pattern]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Analogy: From “sawtooth” dividend pattern to smoothed total return."
- [[section-1031-exchange]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "Casey highlighted that this can be used for various properties (except a primary home) and even for situations like natural disaster losses (1033 exchange)."
- [[section-351-exchange]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "A Section 351 exchange offers a clean, compliant path to swap these appreciated holdings into a diversified ETF without triggering taxes, deferring gains while achieving broad market exposure."
- [[sector-and-country-distortion-under-cap-weights]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "Dot-com bubble and AI surge show tech ballooning in cap-weighted portfolios."
- [[short-term-mean-reversion]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "“There is such a thing as too much of a good thing. Harvesting every day, for example, exposes your portfolio to short-term mean reversion.”"
- [[shorting-against-the-box]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax-aware long-short can require a major restructuring of the household’s equity exposure."
- [[siloed-sleeve-management]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "“Advisors would call me and say, 'Why is there cash in the portfolio? - [[simple-etf-portfolios]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The key argument was that clients can already build simple ETF portfolios themselves."
- [[smart-beta]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "They all fail after tax. Welcome to the real world."
- [[soft-rebalancing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "This is the practice of rebalancing using the portfolio's natural cash flows (dividends, bond coupons, maturities) instead of triggering taxable sales."

- [[state-level-tax-experiments]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "Monitor State-Level Tax Experiments Closely. - [[straddle-rules]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Straddle rules (post‑1984 stock): Call premium taxable short-term currently; put losses non-deductible, added to basis until shares are sold."
- [[structural-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "As Speaker 4 said: “At the end of the day, the benefit is what we call structural alpha.”"
- [[sunset-clause]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "This chapter details the more recent, multi-year campaign to make key pro-growth policies permanent, specifically the full deductibility of R&D and capital investment costs, which were initially left with sunset clauses in the TCJA."
- [[synthetic-rebalancing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "A strategy for holders of large stock positions (e.g., pre-IPO shares) to use options to collar their position, borrow against it at low synthetic rates, and immediately diversify into other assets, all while deferring the initial tax bill."

- [[tax-aware-long-short-implementation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Select managers with demonstrable pretax alpha because alpha powers tax outcomes."
- [[tax-aware-public-market-solutions]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "Before diving into complex alternatives, it's crucial to master the foundational elements of after-tax investing."
- [[tax-breakeven-equation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "As an over-the-counter market, round-trip costs must be factored into the tax-breakeven equation."
- [[tax-design]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "**Tax design** is the deliberate, proactive, engineered approach to minimizing tax drag and improving family-level outcomes."
- [[tax-drag]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "**Tax drag** is the difference between the pre-tax return of an asset or portfolio and the after-tax return the client actually experiences."
- [[tax-engine]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "This keynote fills that gap with 21 rolling 15-year simulations and a framework that redefines how investors should evaluate manager skill: alpha is not just return—it’s a tax engine."
- [[tax-free-policy-loan]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Practicalities: Institutionally priced, skinny policies; no surrender charges; liquidity mirrors underlying assets; tax-free policy loans during life."
- [[tax-loss-harvesting]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax-loss harvesting is often discussed mechanically, but Aaron reframed it more powerfully."
- [[tax-neutral-transitions]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "By using tools like those developed by Brevan Howard and now part of Nuveen, we can create customized, tax-neutral transitions that de-risk a portfolio, defer or offset massive tax bills, and methodically move a client toward their ideal asset allocation."
- [[tax-overlay]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Active models with overlays: meaningful tax alpha when tax overlay is added"
- [[tax-overlay]] → [[_Inbox/at this tax conference Keynote_ After-Tax Wealth Management and Tax Stack Design-Summary (1)]]: "Build a solution inside the after-tax implementation ecosystem. Automate as much as possible. Allow the advisor to use judgment on which levers to pull."

- [[operational-costs]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-presentation_-tax-alpha-dual-share-class-and-tokenized-t‑bills-summ_8e3714]]: "The firm pioneered dual share class structures that deliver ETF trading efficiency to mutual fund buyers and is pushing forward with tokenization to collapse operational costs, tighten spreads, and unlock 24/7 liquidity—all while preserving or improving tax outcomes."

- [[permanent-tax-avoidance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The speaker reveals that unlike equities, where tax loss harvesting is primarily a *deferral* strategy, bond tax loss harvesting can achieve permanent *tax avoidance*."

- [[portfolio-lock-up]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "In contrast, higher alpha in long-only direct indexing tends to lock portfolios up—raising built-in gains and reducing tax-loss harvesting opportunities."
- [[portfolio-risk]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Tax decisions are never purely tax decisions. Selling, holding, transitioning, or hedging all change the risk profile."
- [[position-creation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Definition: Alpha-driven under-leverage in long-short that necessitates adding fresh long and short positions to restore target leverage."
- [[pre-tax-product-selection]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "The keynote’s central theme was the evolution of wealth management from **pre-tax product selection** to **after-tax portfolio design**."
- [[private-placement-life-insurance]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-panel-discussion_-variable-prepaid-forwards-tax-aware-long-short-an_58d676]]: "Core idea: PPLI solves the “back half” of the tax problem—deferring taxes during accumulation, enabling tax-free distribution at death."
- [[property-tax-repeal]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "On the right, some states are exploring the radical idea of repealing property taxes."
- [[proxy-fund]] → [[at this tax conference Presentation_ Tax Alpha, Dual Share Class, and Tokenized T‑Bills-Summary]]: "Strategy avoids dividend capture, rotates into proxy funds around ex-dates, then reverses—collecting price appreciation while minimizing distributions."
- [[qualified-business-income-deduction]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "**QBI Deduction:** Investors can deduct 20% of dividends earned via the Qualified Business Income (QBI) deduction, which is now a permanent part of the tax code."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "A skilled manager can construct a portfolio that delivers a high percentage of its distribution as QDI, making its taxable equivalent yield extremely attractive."
- [[qualified-dividend-income]] → [[at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]]: "She explained that about half of the preferreds universe throws off Qualified Dividend Income (QDI), which is taxed at a much lower rate (0-20%)."
- [[r&d-expensing]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-us-tax-policy-partisanship-populism-and-protectionism-summ_cd29d7]]: "This chapter details the more recent, multi-year campaign to make key pro-growth policies permanent, specifically the full deductibility of R&D and capital investment costs, which were initially left with sunset clauses in the TCJA."
- [[realized-gains-and-losses]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-after-tax-wealth-management-and-tax-stack-design-summary-1_b17566]]: "Aaron made an important distinction: **tax drag is not a single problem**. It is a collection of problems that compound into complexity."
- [[rebalancing-alpha]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fundamental-indexing-rafi-and-rebalancing-alpha-_a509e2]]: "When the market is wrong, this systematic contrarian rebalancing earns **rebalancing alpha.**"
- [[rebalancing-destination]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "Once the trigger is hit (e.g., at 66.5% equity), you don't rebalance all the way back to 60%."
- [[rebalancing-tax-drag]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-alpha-fixed-income-and-smart-rebalancing-summary_58815e]]: "The friction of rebalancing creates a significant, unmeasured tax drag that can be drastically reduced by managing the portfolio holistically, not in sleeves."
- [[research-gap]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "Despite a robust literature on taxes and portfolio mechanics, no prior work had directly measured how pretax alpha affects tax outcomes in long-short contexts."
- [[rolling-15-year-simulation]] → [[sources/at this tax conference-keynote_-tax-aware-long-short-investing-pretax-alpha-and-after-tax-_108d27]]: "This keynote fills that gap with 21 rolling 15-year simulations and a framework that redefines how investors should evaluate manager skill: alpha is not just return—it’s a tax engine."

### Duplicate pages (detected)

- [[10-09-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_80024f]] and [[10-09-keynote_-after-tax-investing-tax-neutral-transitions-and-private-re_daa283]] — Same source document: both pages are summaries of the identical keynote presentation on after-tax investing, tax-neutral transitions, and private real estate strategies.
- [[concentrated-stock-position]] and [[concentrated-wealth]] — Same concept: both describe large, low-basis single-stock holdings carrying idiosyncratic downside risk and tax lock-in, functioning as direct aliases for each other.
- [[delaware-statutory-trust]] and [[delaware-statutory-trust-dst]] — Same concept/entity: both refer to the Delaware Statutory Trust (DST) as an intermediate investment structure used in Section 1031 like-kind exchanges and upREIT transitions.
- [[sweet-riley]] and [[riley]] — Same entity: both refer to Herr Schmaltz's widow (Riley / Sweet Riley) who held the concentrated Wachovia bank stock position through the 2008 financial crisis.
- [[tax-aware-long-short-equity]] and [[tax-aware-long-short-implementation]] — Same concept: both refer to the tax-aware long-short (TALS) equity investment strategy and portfolio implementation designed to systematically harvest capital loss inventory.
